Per-file role inventory - all paths relative to the package root.
Hash verification is provided separately by SUBMISSION_MANIFEST.json.

LOCALIZATION.json
  Class: source_integrity
  Purpose: Maps original evidence to English presentation paths and fingerprints; preserve unchanged.
  Retention: Keep for existing verification, scientific source correspondence or full reproduction.

PACKAGE_MANIFEST.json
  Class: source_integrity
  Purpose: Historical EN1 package inventory for its original 1,179 entries; not the current delivery inventory.
  Retention: Keep for existing verification, scientific source correspondence or full reproduction.

README.md
  Class: current_guide
  Purpose: Current start, commands, platform limits, evidence locations and qualified provenance.
  Retention: Keep for its documented evidence, navigation, historical or verification role.

README.txt
  Class: historical_guide
  Purpose: Retained older handoff manual; superseded as the current entry by README.md.
  Retention: Keep for its documented evidence, navigation, historical or verification role.

START_HERE.txt
  Class: current_guide
  Purpose: Short entry to the current README, saved viewer and completed review evidence.
  Retention: Keep for its documented evidence, navigation, historical or verification role.

SUBMISSION_MANIFEST.json
  Class: current_integrity
  Purpose: Current file-hash inventory used by submission.py verify; excludes itself.
  Retention: Keep for its documented evidence, navigation, historical or verification role.

audit/README.txt
  Class: historical_guide
  Purpose: Explains the original archive and the boundary between English guides and immutable Chinese evidence.
  Retention: Keep for its documented evidence, navigation, historical or verification role.

audit/original_evidence.zip
  Class: original_evidence
  Purpose: Immutable nested original handoff, including original names, records and frozen-source checks.
  Retention: Keep for its documented evidence, navigation, historical or verification role.

case_analysis/README.txt

  Retention: Keep for its documented evidence, navigation, historical or verification role.
